{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ANwEoiTE1Vf6XXK76uQMkj","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# Architectural Decision Record: 001 — Adopt Hexagonal Architecture (Ports & Adapters)\n     3→\n     4→| Status | Date | Deciders |  \n     5→| ------ | ---- | -------- |  \n     6→| Accepted | 2024-06-11 | Core Architecture Team (A. Dev, B. Lead, C. Ops) |\n     7→\n     8→## 1. Context  \n     9→\n    10→SprintCart Pro is positioned as an _operator-first_ e-commerce platform where every back-office interaction is a measurable, optimizable work cycle.  \n    11→Key business drivers:\n    12→\n    13→* Rapid feature velocity — new sales channels (live-shopping, voice assistants, AI chat) must be added without re-writing the business core.  \n    14→* On-prem & SaaS deployments — domain logic must remain agnostic to external infrastructure (RDBMS vs. document store, single-tenant vs. multi-tenant).  \n    15→* High-performance back office — productivity tooling (e.g., bulk editors, automation studio) must call the same domain use-cases as the public storefront to guarantee consistency.  \n    16→\n    17→After evaluating several architectural styles (Layered, Clean Architecture, Event-Sourced, Micro-Kernels), we need an approach that:\n    18→\n    19→1. Keeps domain code pure and testable.  \n    20→2. Decouples inbound adapters (REST, GraphQL, SPA, CLI, batch) from outbound integrations (Stripe, SendGrid, ERP) through explicit, technology-agnostic contracts.  \n    21→3. Minimizes cross-cutting concerns bleeding into business logic (e.g., tracing, auth, validation).  \n    22→\n    23→## 2. Decision  \n    24→\n    25→We will structure SprintCart Pro following _Hexagonal Architecture_ (Ports & Adapters) as described by Alistair Cockburn:\n    26→\n    27→* **Domain Layer (Inside)** — Aggregates, Entities, Value Objects, and Domain Services with no dependencies on frameworks.  \n    28→* **Application Layer** — Use-cases (Service Layer) orchestrating domain objects via _inbound ports_ (commands/queries).  \n    29→* **Inbound Adapters** — REST/GraphQL controllers, WebSocket handlers, scheduled jobs.  \n    30→* **Outbound Adapters** — Payment gateways, messaging, notification services, external ERPs.  \n    31→* **Ports** — Java interfaces owned by the core that define required/expected behavior, enabling adapters to plug in without touching the domain.  \n    32→\n    33→### Folder Blueprint (Gradle multi-module)\n    34→\n    35→```\n    36→sprintcart-pro/\n    37→ ├─ sprintcart-domain/          // pure Java/Kotlin, no Spring\n    38→ ├─ sprintcart-application/     // use-cases, ports\n    39→ ├─ sprintcart-adapter-in/      // web-rest, graphql, scheduler\n    40→ ├─ sprintcart-adapter-out/     // stripe, sendgrid, kafka\n    41→ └─ sprintcart-boot/            // Spring Boot composition root\n    42→```\n    43→\n    44→## 3. Consequences  \n    45→\n    46→### Positive\n    47→* Domain logic is testable with plain JUnit; adapters are mocked via ports.  \n    48→* New channels (voice POS, headless kiosk) add an inbound adapter without touching core.  \n    49→* Payment gateways can be swapped (Stripe → Adyen) by implementing the same `PaymentPort`.  \n    50→* Clear ownership boundaries reduce merge conflicts across teams (Core vs. Integration).  \n    51→\n    52→### Negative\n    53→* Boilerplate overhead for simple CRUD scenarios.  \n    54→* Developers unfamiliar with Ports & Adapters need onboarding.  \n    55→* Extra indirection can complicate debugging if logging/tracing is not standardized.  \n    56→\n    57→## 4. Sample Reference Implementation  \n    58→\n    59→```java\n    60→// sprintcart-application/src/main/java/pro/sprintcart/checkout/port/in/PlaceOrderCommand.java\n    61→package pro.sprintcart.checkout.port.in;\n    62→\n    63→import java.util.UUID;\n    64→\n    65→/**\n    66→ * Inbound Port exposed by the Application layer.\n    67→ * Technology-agnostic contract for placing an order.\n    68→ */\n    69→public interface PlaceOrderCommand {\n    70→    UUID place(OrderDraft draft);\n    71→}\n    72→```\n    73→\n    74→```java\n    75→// sprintcart-application/src/main/java/pro/sprintcart/checkout/port/out/PaymentPort.java\n    76→package pro.sprintcart.checkout.port.out;\n    77→\n    78→import pro.sprintcart.checkout.domain.Payment;\n    79→import pro.sprintcart.checkout.domain.PaymentMethod;\n    80→\n    81→/**\n    82→ * Outbound Port required by the core.\n    83→ * Implemented by StripeAdapter, AdyenAdapter, etc.\n    84→ */\n    85→public interface PaymentPort {\n    86→    Payment charge(PaymentMethod method, Money amount);\n    87→}\n    88→```\n    89→\n    90→```java\n    91→// sprintcart-adapter-out/stripe/StripePaymentAdapter.java\n    92→package pro.sprintcart.adapter.out.stripe;\n    93→\n    94→import com.stripe.exception.StripeException;\n    95→import com.stripe.model.Charge;\n    96→import pro.sprintcart.checkout.domain.*;\n    97→import pro.sprintcart.checkout.port.out.PaymentPort;\n    98→import pro.sprintcart.shared.Money;\n    99→import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;\n   100→\n   101→@Component\n   102→public class StripePaymentAdapter implements PaymentPort {\n   103→\n   104→    @Override\n   105→    public Payment charge(PaymentMethod method, Money amount) {\n   106→        try {\n   107→            Charge charge = Charge.create(Map.of(\n   108→                \"amount\", amount.inMinorUnits(),\n   109→                \"currency\", amount.getCurrency().getCurrencyCode(),\n   110→                \"source\", method.token()\n   111→            ));\n   112→            return Payment.approved(charge.getId());\n   113→        } catch (StripeException ex) {\n   114→            throw new PaymentGatewayException(\"Stripe charge failed\", ex);\n   115→        }\n   116→    }\n   117→}\n   118→```\n   119→\n   120→```java\n   121→// sprintcart-adapter-in/rest/CheckoutController.java\n   122→package pro.sprintcart.adapter.in.rest;\n   123→\n   124→import jakarta.validation.Valid;\n   125→import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;\n   126→import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;\n   127→import pro.sprintcart.checkout.port.in.PlaceOrderCommand;\n   128→import pro.sprintcart.checkout.application.dto.OrderDraftDTO;\n   129→\n   130→@RestController\n   131→@RequestMapping(\"/api/v1/checkout\")\n   132→public class CheckoutController {\n   133→\n   134→    private final PlaceOrderCommand placeOrder;\n   135→\n   136→    public CheckoutController(PlaceOrderCommand placeOrder) {\n   137→        this.placeOrder = placeOrder;\n   138→    }\n   139→\n   140→    @PostMapping(\"/orders\")\n   141→    public ResponseEntity<?> place(@Valid @RequestBody OrderDraftDTO dto) {\n   142→        var orderId = placeOrder.place(dto.toDomain());\n   143→        return ResponseEntity.accepted().body(Map.of(\"orderId\", orderId));\n   144→    }\n   145→}\n   146→```\n   147→\n   148→The domain, application, and adapter layers compile independently, are wired by Spring Boot in the `sprintcart-boot` module, and can be sliced for serverless or on-prem deployments.\n   149→\n   150→## 5. References  \n   151→\n   152→* Alistair Cockburn — _Hexagonal Architecture_.  \n   153→* “Ports and Adapters Architecture” — Fowler, M. Patterns of Enterprise Application Architecture.  \n   154→* Team ADR 000 — Directory structure and ADR decision-making policy.  \n   155→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. Dev, B. Lead, C. Ops) |\n\n## 1. Context  \n\nSprintCart Pro is positioned as an _operator-first_ e-commerce platform where every back-office interaction is a measurable, optimizable work cycle.  \nKey business drivers:\n\n* Rapid feature velocity — new sales channels (live-shopping, voice assistants, AI chat) must be added without re-writing the business core.  \n* On-prem & SaaS deployments — domain logic must remain agnostic to external infrastructure (RDBMS vs. document store, single-tenant vs. multi-tenant).  \n* High-performance back office — productivity tooling (e.g., bulk editors, automation studio) must call the same domain use-cases as the public storefront to guarantee consistency.  \n\nAfter evaluating several architectural styles (Layered, Clean Architecture, Event-Sourced, Micro-Kernels), we need an approach that:\n\n1. Keeps domain code pure and testable.  \n2. Decouples inbound adapters (REST, GraphQL, SPA, CLI, batch) from outbound integrations (Stripe, SendGrid, ERP) through explicit, technology-agnostic contracts.  \n3. Minimizes cross-cutting concerns bleeding into business logic (e.g., tracing, auth, validation).  \n\n## 2. Decision  \n\nWe will structure SprintCart Pro following _Hexagonal Architecture_ (Ports & Adapters) as described by Alistair Cockburn:\n\n* **Domain Layer (Inside)** — Aggregates, Entities, Value Objects, and Domain Services with no dependencies on frameworks.  \n* **Application Layer** — Use-cases (Service Layer) orchestrating domain objects via _inbound ports_ (commands/queries).  \n* **Inbound Adapters** — REST/GraphQL controllers, WebSocket handlers, scheduled jobs.  \n* **Outbound Adapters** — Payment gateways, messaging, notification services, external ERPs.  \n* **Ports** — Java interfaces owned by the core that define required/expected behavior, enabling adapters to plug in without touching the domain.  \n\n### Folder Blueprint (Gradle multi-module)\n\n```\nsprintcart-pro/\n ├─ sprintcart-domain/          // pure Java/Kotlin, no Spring\n ├─ sprintcart-application/     // use-cases, ports\n ├─ sprintcart-adapter-in/      // web-rest, graphql, scheduler\n ├─ sprintcart-adapter-out/     // stripe, sendgrid, kafka\n └─ sprintcart-boot/            // Spring Boot composition root\n```\n\n## 3. Consequences  \n\n### Positive\n* Domain logic is testable with plain JUnit; adapters are mocked via ports.  \n* New channels (voice POS, headless kiosk) add an inbound adapter without touching core.  \n* Payment gateways can be swapped (Stripe → Adyen) by implementing the same `PaymentPort`.  \n* Clear ownership boundaries reduce merge conflicts across teams (Core vs. Integration).  \n\n### Negative\n* Boilerplate overhead for simple CRUD scenarios.  \n* Developers unfamiliar with Ports & Adapters need onboarding.  \n* Extra indirection can complicate debugging if logging/tracing is not standardized.  \n\n## 4. Sample Reference Implementation  \n\n```java\n// sprintcart-application/src/main/java/pro/sprintcart/checkout/port/in/PlaceOrderCommand.java\npackage pro.sprintcart.checkout.port.in;\n\nimport java.util.UUID;\n\n/**\n * Inbound Port exposed by the Application layer.\n * Technology-agnostic contract for placing an order.\n */\npublic interface PlaceOrderCommand {\n    UUID place(OrderDraft draft);\n}\n```\n\n```java\n// sprintcart-application/src/main/java/pro/sprintcart/checkout/port/out/PaymentPort.java\npackage pro.sprintcart.checkout.port.out;\n\nimport pro.sprintcart.checkout.domain.Payment;\nimport pro.sprintcart.checkout.domain.PaymentMethod;\n\n/**\n * Outbound Port required by the core.\n * Implemented by StripeAdapter, AdyenAdapter, etc.\n */\npublic interface PaymentPort {\n    Payment charge(PaymentMethod method, Money amount);\n}\n```\n\n```java\n// sprintcart-adapter-out/stripe/StripePaymentAdapter.java\npackage pro.sprintcart.adapter.out.stripe;\n\nimport com.stripe.exception.StripeException;\nimport com.stripe.model.Charge;\nimport pro.sprintcart.checkout.domain.*;\nimport pro.sprintcart.checkout.port.out.PaymentPort;\nimport pro.sprintcart.shared.Money;\nimport org.springframework.stereotype.Component;\n\n@Component\npublic class StripePaymentAdapter implements PaymentPort {\n\n    @Override\n    public Payment charge(PaymentMethod method, Money amount) {\n        try {\n            Charge charge = Charge.create(Map.of(\n                \"amount\", amount.inMinorUnits(),\n                \"currency\", amount.getCurrency().getCurrencyCode(),\n                \"source\", method.token()\n            ));\n            return Payment.approved(charge.getId());\n        } catch (StripeException ex) {\n            throw new PaymentGatewayException(\"Stripe charge failed\", ex);\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n```java\n// sprintcart-adapter-in/rest/CheckoutController.java\npackage pro.sprintcart.adapter.in.rest;\n\nimport jakarta.validation.Valid;\nimport org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;\nimport org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;\nimport pro.sprintcart.checkout.port.in.PlaceOrderCommand;\nimport pro.sprintcart.checkout.application.dto.OrderDraftDTO;\n\n@RestController\n@RequestMapping(\"/api/v1/checkout\")\npublic class CheckoutController {\n\n    private final PlaceOrderCommand placeOrder;\n\n    public CheckoutController(PlaceOrderCommand placeOrder) {\n        this.placeOrder = placeOrder;\n    }\n\n    @PostMapping(\"/orders\")\n    public ResponseEntity<?> place(@Valid @RequestBody OrderDraftDTO dto) {\n        var orderId = placeOrder.place(dto.toDomain());\n        return ResponseEntity.accepted().body(Map.of(\"orderId\", orderId));\n    }\n}\n```\n\nThe domain, application, and adapter layers compile independently, are wired by Spring Boot in the `sprintcart-boot` module, and can be sliced for serverless or on-prem deployments.\n\n## 5. References  \n\n* Alistair Cockburn — _Hexagonal Architecture_.  \n* “Ports and Adapters Architecture” — Fowler, M. Patterns of Enterprise Application Architecture.  \n* Team ADR 000 — Directory structure and ADR decision-making policy.  \n```","numLines":155,"startLine":1,"totalLines":155}}}

Validate & reserve stock in a best-effort manner\n        List<OrderItem> requestedItems = command.toOrderItems();\n        if (!inventoryPort.reserveStock(requestedItems)) {\n            log.warn(\"Stock reservation failed for customer={}\", customerId);\n            throw new BusinessViolation(\"One or more items are out of stock\");\n        }\n\n        // 2. Domain state transition\n            order.markAsPaid(clock);\n            orderCommandPort.save(order);\n\n            // 5. metricsPort.incrementCounter(\"order.cancelled\");\n        log.info(\"Order {} cancelled by user {}\", orderId, requestedBy);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Web-hook entry-point used by the payment Must be tuned\n     * according to traffic; defaults to CPU*2 thread-pool in Spring Boot.\n     */\n    private final AsyncTaskExecutor taskExecutor;\n\n    private final Clock clock = Clock.systemUTC();\n\n    @Override\n    public void afterPropertiesSet() {\n        log.info(\"DomainEventProcessor initialized with {} subscriber(s)\",\n                subscribers.size());\n        subscribers.forEach(s ->\n                log.debug(\"   ↳ {}\", s.getClass().getName()));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handles events published via {@code ApplicationEventPublisher}. An individual subscriber\n     * failure does <strong>not</strong> prevent other subscribers from running.\n     */\n    private <E extends DomainEvent> void dispatchAsync(\n            DomainEventSubscriber<E> subscriber, E event) {\n\n        CompletableFuture\n            .runAsync(() -> invokeHandler(subscriber, event), taskExecutor)\n            .exceptionally(ex -> {\n                log.error(\"❌ Subscriber '{}' failed for event {}\",\n                        subscriber.getClass().getSimpleName(), event.eventId(), ex);\n                // In a real system we would publish to a DLQ here\n                return null;\n            });\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Invokes subscriber handler with proper error boundaries. Wrapped into its\n     * own method to produce cleaner stack traces inside {@link CompletableFuture}.\n     */\n    private <E extends DomainEvent> void invokeHandler(\n            DomainEventSubscriber<E> subscriber, E event) {\n\n        try {\n            subscriber.handle(event);\n            log.debug(\"↳ {} handled by {}\", event.getClass().getSimpleName(),\n                    subscriber.getClass().getSimpleName());\n        } catch (DataAccessException dae) {\n            // Non-transient DB error – rethrow to trigger retry/back-off\n            throw dae;\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            // Shield rest of the pipeline; mark failure for DLQ\n            log.error(\"Subscriber {} threw exception for event {}\",\n                    subscriber.getClass().getName(), event.eventId(), e);\n            throw e;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Ensures that the in-memory journal does not leak after container\n     * shutdown. A real implementation would not be necessary because the\n     * journal would live in an external store.\n     */\n    @PreDestroy\n    public void teardown() {\n        processedEventJournal.clear();\n        log.info(\"DomainEventProcessor stopped – journal cleared\");\n    }\n\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Nested Types\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /**\n     * A marker interface for all domain events in SprintCart Pro.\n     * Extend this in <code>domain.events.*</code> packages.\n     */\n    public interface DomainEvent extends Serializable {\n        @Serial\n        long serialVersionUID = 1L;\n\n        /**\n         * Globally unique identifier that guarantees idempotency.\n         */\n        @NonNull UUID eventId();\n\n        /**\n         * Timestamp of occurrence (UTC). Helps for latency metrics.\n         */\n        @NonNull Instant occurredOn();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Contract to be implemented by application services that react to a\n     * specific type of domain event. Generic to enforce type-safety.\n     *\n     * @param <E> concrete event class\n     */\n    public interface DomainEventSubscriber<E extends DomainEvent> {\n\n        /**\n         * Handle the event. May throw a runtime exception; the processor will\n         * catch and log it without bringing down the JVM.\n         */\n        void handle(@NonNull E event);\n\n        /**\n         * Whether this subscriber accepts the given event type.\n         * Most implementations return <code>eventType.equals(MyEvent.class)</code>.\n         */\n        boolean supports(@NonNull Class<? extends DomainEvent> eventType);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Wrapper that allows publishing {@link DomainEvent}s through Spring's\n     * {@code ApplicationEventPublisher} while keeping our domain model free\n     * from framework dependencies.\n     */\n    public record SpringDomainEvent(DomainEvent event) implements Serializable {\n        @Serial\n        private static final long serialVersionUID = 42L;\n    }\n\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Convenience utilities\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /**\n     * Utility to publish domain events via Spring without depending on\n     * {@code ApplicationEventPublisher} outside this class.\n     *\n     * Example:\n     * <pre>\n     *   DomainEventProcessor.publish(applicationContext, new OrderCreatedEvent(...));\n     * </pre>\n     */\n    public static void publish(\n            @NonNull org.springframework.context.ApplicationEventPublisher publisher,\n            @NonNull DomainEvent event) {\n\n        Objects.requireNonNull(publisher, \"publisher must not be null\");\n        Objects.requireNonNull(event, \"event must not be null\");\n\n        publisher.publishEvent(new SpringDomainEvent(event));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Optional helper that can be used by repositories or aggregates to create\n     * a default UUID for events when none is provided.\n     */\n    public static UUID randomEventId() {\n        return UUID.randomUUID();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Safely extracts the value of an {@link Optional}, throwing\n     * {@link IllegalStateException} when empty. Reduces null checks across\n     * subscribers while still keeping failure explicit.\n     */\n    public static <T> T requirePresent(Optional<T> optional, String message) {\n        if (optional.isEmpty()) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(message);\n        }\n        return optional.get();\n    }\n}\n```","numLines":273,"startLine":1,"totalLines":273}}}

Retrieve & validate the Order in a transaction-safe manner.\n *  2. Reserve inventory and record shortages.\n *  3. Generate a picking list and corresponding shipment label.\n *  4. Persist Order state transitions (Fulfilling -> Fulfilled).\n *  5. Emit domain events and user notifications.\n */\n@Slf4j\n@Service\n@RequiredArgsConstructor\npublic class FulfillmentService {\n\n    // ————————————————————————————————————————————————————————— Ports & Dependencies\n    private final OrderRepository orderRepository;\n    private final InventoryGateway inventoryGateway;\n    private final ShippingGateway shippingGateway;\n    private final NotificationGateway notificationGateway;\n    private final DomainEventPublisher eventPublisher;\n    private final TimeProvider timeProvider;\n\n    private static final int MAX_RETRIES_ON_CONTENTION = 3;\n\n    /**\n     * Triggers the full fulfillment workflow for a single order.\n     *\n     * @param orderId    Business identifier of the order.\n     * @param operatorId User who initiated the action (auditing / productivity KPI).\n     * @return An immutable value object describing the outcome.\n     */\n    public FulfillmentResult fulfill(UUID orderId, OperatorId operatorId) {\n        Instant startedAt = timeProvider.now();\n        AtomicInteger attemptCounter = new AtomicInteger(0);\n\n        while (attemptCounter.incrementAndGet() <= MAX_RETRIES_ON_CONTENTION) {\n            try {\n                return fulfillInternal(orderId, operatorId, startedAt);\n            } catch (OptimisticLockingFailureException e) {\n                log.warn(\"Contention detected while trying to fulfill order {} (attempt {}/{})\",\n                        orderId, attemptCounter.get(), MAX_RETRIES_ON_CONTENTION);\n                sleepBackOff(attemptCounter.get());\n            }\n        }\n        throw new FulfillmentException(\"Could not fulfill order \" + orderId + \" due to repeated concurrent modifications\");\n    }\n\n    // ——————————————————————————————————————————————————————————————— Internal\n\n    @Transactional\n    protected FulfillmentResult fulfillInternal(UUID orderId,\n                                                OperatorId operatorId,\n                                                Instant startedAt) {\n\n        Order order = orderRepository.findById(orderId)\n                .orElseThrow(() -> new FulfillmentException(\"Order \" + orderId + \" not found\"));\n\n        if (order.getStatus() != OrderStatus.PAID) {\n            throw new BusinessRuleViolationException(\"Only orders in PAID state can be fulfilled\");\n        }\n\n        // 1. Reserve inventory\n        InventoryReservationSummary reservationSummary = reserveInventory(order);\n\n        // 2. Create picking list (domain object for warehouse employees)\n        PickingList pickingList = generatePickingList(order, reservationSummary);\n\n        // 3. Ship (interacts with external carrier API)\n        ShipmentLabel shipmentLabel = generateShipmentLabel(order, pickingList);\n\n        // 4. Transition order state\n        order.markAsFulfilled(shipmentLabel.trackingNumber(), timeProvider.now());\n        orderRepository.save(order);\n\n        // 5. Emit domain events & notifications\n        eventPublisher.publish(new OrderFulfilledEvent(order.getId(), operatorId, shipmentLabel.trackingNumber()));\n        notificationGateway.notifyCustomerFulfilled(order.getCustomerId(), order.getId(), shipmentLabel);\n\n        Duration totalDuration = Duration.between(startedAt, timeProvider.now());\n        log.info(\"Order {} fulfilled in {} ms\", orderId, totalDuration.toMillis());\n\n        return new FulfillmentResult(orderId,\n                                     shipmentLabel.trackingNumber(),\n                                     reservationSummary,\n                                     totalDuration);\n    }\n\n    // —————————————————————————————————————————————————————————— Sub-Steps\n\n    private InventoryReservationSummary reserveInventory(Order order) {\n        InventoryReservationSummary summary = new InventoryReservationSummary();\n\n        for (OrderLine line : order.getOrderLines()) {\n            StockReservationResult result =\n                    inventoryGateway.reserveStock(line.getSku(), line.getQuantity());\n\n            summary.add(result);\n\n            if (!result.isSuccessful()) {\n                log.warn(\"Partial stock reservation for order {} – SKU {} only {} reserved out of {}\",\n                        order.getId(), line.getSku(), result.reserved(), line.getQuantity());\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (!summary.isFullyReserved()) {\n            throw new InsufficientStockException(order.getId(), summary);\n        }\n        return summary;\n    }\n\n    private PickingList generatePickingList(Order order, InventoryReservationSummary summary) {\n        List<PickingItem> items = order.getOrderLines().stream()\n                .map(ol -> new PickingItem(ol.getSku(), ol.getQuantity(), summary.getLocatorFor(ol.getSku())))\n                .toList();\n\n        return new PickingList(order.getId(), items, order.getShippingAddress());\n    }\n\n    private ShipmentLabel generateShipmentLabel(Order order, PickingList pickingList) {\n        Address shipTo = order.getShippingAddress();\n        return shippingGateway.createShipment(order.getId(), shipTo, pickingList.totalWeight());\n    }\n\n    // —————————————————————————————————————————————————————————— Utilities\n\n    private void sleepBackOff(int attempt) {\n        try {\n            Thread.sleep(200L * attempt); // simple linear back-off\n        } catch (InterruptedException ie) {\n            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ——————————————————————————————————————————————————————————— Types\n\n    /**\n     * DTO representing the outcome of the fulfillment process.\n     * Could later be extended with analytics metadata.\n     */\n    public record FulfillmentResult(UUID orderId,\n                                    String trackingNumber,\n                                    InventoryReservationSummary reservationSummary,\n                                    Duration processingTime) {\n    }\n}","numLines":177,"startLine":1,"totalLines":177}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_01XLSYsu9Vdy9icwxJYMVsDV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01MohgCciauyzRrwLE6AUzqh","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/logs/agent/solution.md","content":"# Technical Plan: Warehouse Management System (WMS) Integration for SprintCart Pro\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis document presents a detailed technical plan for integrating a third-party Warehouse Management System (WMS) into the SprintCart Pro e-commerce platform. The integration supports two critical business capabilities:\n\n1. **Outbound Fulfillment Request**: When an order is successfully paid and confirmed, SprintCart Pro sends fulfillment details to the WMS API.\n2. **Inbound Stock Update**: SprintCart Pro exposes a secure endpoint for the WMS to push real-time stock level updates.\n\nThis plan strictly adheres to the Hexagonal Architecture (Ports & Adapters) pattern established in ADR-001 and maintains consistency with existing C4 model documentation.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Module and Dependency Strategy\n\n### 1.1 New Maven Module Required\n\nA new adapter module should be created under the `sprintcart-pro-adapters` parent:\n\n```\nsprintcart-pro-adapters/\n├── notification-adapter/\n├── payment-adapter/\n├── persistence-adapter/\n├── search-adapter/\n├── web-adapter/\n└── wms-adapter/          <-- NEW MODULE\n```\n\n**Module Name:** `sprintcart-pro-adapters-wms`\n**Artifact ID:** `wms-adapter`\n\n### 1.2 Module Dependencies\n\n#### New `wms-adapter` Module Dependencies\n\n```xml\n<dependencies>\n    <!-- Internal Hexagonal Layers -->\n    <dependency>\n        <groupId>com.sprintcart.pro</groupId>\n        <artifactId>sprintcart-pro-domain</artifactId>\n        <version>${project.version}</version>\n    </dependency>\n    <dependency>\n        <groupId>com.sprintcart.pro</groupId>\n        <artifactId>sprintcart-pro-application</artifactId>\n        <version>${project.version}</version>\n    </dependency>\n\n    <!-- HTTP Client for outbound WMS calls -->\n    <dependency>\n        <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>\n        <artifactId>spring-boot-starter-webflux</artifactId>\n    </dependency>\n\n    <!-- REST endpoint for inbound WMS webhooks -->\n    <dependency>\n        <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>\n        <artifactId>spring-boot-starter-web</artifactId>\n    </dependency>\n\n    <!-- Security for webhook authentication -->\n    <dependency>\n        <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>\n        <artifactId>spring-boot-starter-security</artifactId>\n    </dependency>\n\n    <!-- Validation -->\n    <dependency>\n        <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>\n        <artifactId>spring-boot-starter-validation</artifactId>\n    </dependency>\n\n    <!-- Resilience patterns (circuit breaker, retry) -->\n    <dependency>\n        <groupId>io.github.resilience4j</groupId>\n        <artifactId>resilience4j-spring-boot3</artifactId>\n    </dependency>\n</dependencies>\n```\n\n#### Dependency Direction (Hexagonal Compliance)\n\nFollowing the dependency rule from ADR-001 where \"dependencies point inwards\":\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                      wms-adapter (new)                          │\n│   ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │\n│   │ WmsController (inbound REST)                             │  │\n│   │ WmsRestClient (outbound HTTP)                            │  │\n│   └────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────┘  │\n│                            │ depends on                         │\n│   ┌────────────────────────▼─────────────────────────────────┐  │\n│   │              sprintcart-pro-application                   │  │\n│   │  (CatalogService, OrderService, DomainEventProcessor)     │  │\n│   └────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────┘  │\n│                            │ depends on                         │\n│   ┌────────────────────────▼─────────────────────────────────┐  │\n│   │              sprintcart-pro-domain                        │  │\n│   │  (WmsPort interfaces, domain events, value objects)       │  │\n│   └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n**Key Constraint:** The `sprintcart-pro-domain` module must NOT depend on any adapter or framework-specific code.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Ports (Interfaces) Definition\n\nFollowing the established pattern in the codebase (see `PaymentGatewayPort`, `ErpSystemPort`, `ManageCatalogUseCase`), we define both **outbound ports** and **inbound ports (use cases)**.\n\n### 2.1 Outbound Port: `WmsPort` (Driven Port)\n\n**Location:** `sprintcart-pro-domain/src/main/java/com/sprintcart/domain/ports/out/wms/WmsPort.java`\n\nThis port abstracts all outbound communication with the WMS. The domain layer calls this interface; the adapter implements it.\n\n```java\npackage com.sprintcart.domain.ports.out.wms;\n\nimport java.time.Duration;\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Objects;\nimport java.util.UUID;\nimport java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;\n\n/**\n * Outbound port that abstracts all interactions with an external Warehouse Management System (WMS).\n * <p>\n * Implementations reside in the infrastructure/adapter layer and handle protocol-specific\n * details (REST, webhooks, message queues). The domain layer depends solely on this interface,\n * ensuring complete decoupling from any particular WMS vendor or transport mechanism.\n *\n * <h3>Resilience</h3>\n * All operations return {@link CompletableFuture}s for non-blocking execution. Implementations\n * are expected to apply timeouts, retries, and circuit breaker patterns. Failures must be\n * propagated as {@link WmsIntegrationException}s.\n */\npublic interface WmsPort {\n\n    /* ========================================================================\n     * Fulfillment Operations (Outbound to WMS)\n     * ======================================================================== */\n\n    /**\n     * Submits a fulfillment request to the WMS for processing.\n     * <p>\n     * This is typically called after an order is paid and ready for shipment.\n     * The WMS will allocate inventory, generate picking lists, and coordinate\n     * the physical fulfillment process.\n     *\n     * @param request Contains order details, shipping address, and line items.\n     * @return Future containing the WMS acknowledgment with external reference.\n     * @throws WmsIntegrationException if communication fails or WMS rejects the request.\n     */\n    CompletableFuture<FulfillmentAcknowledgment> submitFulfillmentRequest(FulfillmentRequest request)\n            throws WmsIntegrationException;\n\n    /**\n     * Queries the current fulfillment status from the WMS.\n     *\n     * @param orderId SprintCart order identifier.\n     * @return Future containing current status or empty if not found in WMS.\n     */\n    CompletableFuture<FulfillmentStatusResponse> getFulfillmentStatus(UUID orderId)\n            throws WmsIntegrationException;\n\n    /**\n     * Cancels an in-progress fulfillment request.\n     *\n     * @param orderId SprintCart order identifier.\n     * @param reason Cancellation reason for audit trail.\n     * @return Future indicating success or failure.\n     */\n    CompletableFuture<Boolean> cancelFulfillment(UUID orderId, String reason)\n            throws WmsIntegrationException;\n\n    /* ========================================================================\n     * Health & Diagnostics\n     * ======================================================================== */\n\n    /**\n     * Performs a lightweight health check against the WMS endpoint.\n     */\n    WmsHealth healthCheck();\n\n    /**\n     * Returns WMS integration metadata for observability dashboards.\n     */\n    WmsMetadata metadata();\n\n    /* ========================================================================\n     * Value Objects & Records\n     * ======================================================================== */\n\n    /**\n     * Request to initiate fulfillment for an order.\n     */\n    record FulfillmentRequest(\n            UUID orderId,\n            UUID customerId,\n            String correlationId,\n            ShippingDetails shippingDetails,\n            List<FulfillmentLineItem> lineItems,\n            FulfillmentPriority priority,\n            Instant requestedAt\n    ) {\n        public FulfillmentRequest {\n            Objects.requireNonNull(orderId, \"orderId must not be null\");\n            Objects.requireNonNull(customerId, \"customerId must not be null\");\n            Objects.requireNonNull(shippingDetails, \"shippingDetails must not be null\");\n            Objects.requireNonNull(lineItems, \"lineItems must not be null\");\n            if (lineItems.isEmpty()) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"lineItems must not be empty\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Individual line item within a fulfillment request.\n     */\n    record FulfillmentLineItem(\n            UUID productId,\n            String sku,\n            String productName,\n            int quantity,\n            String specialInstructions\n    ) {\n        public FulfillmentLineItem {\n            Objects.requireNonNull(productId, \"productId must not be null\");\n            Objects.requireNonNull(sku, \"sku must not be null\");\n            if (quantity <= 0) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"quantity must be positive\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Shipping destination details.\n     */\n    record ShippingDetails(\n            String recipientName,\n            String addressLine1,\n            String addressLine2,\n            String city,\n            String state,\n            String postalCode,\n            String country,\n            String phoneNumber,\n            String shippingMethod\n    ) {}\n\n    /**\n     * Priority level for fulfillment processing.\n     */\n    enum FulfillmentPriority {\n        STANDARD,\n        EXPRESS,\n        SAME_DAY,\n        PRIORITY\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Acknowledgment returned by WMS after accepting a fulfillment request.\n     */\n    record FulfillmentAcknowledgment(\n            String wmsReferenceId,\n            UUID orderId,\n            FulfillmentStatus status,\n            Instant acceptedAt,\n            Instant estimatedShipDate\n    ) {}\n\n    /**\n     * Current fulfillment status from WMS.\n     */\n    record FulfillmentStatusResponse(\n            UUID orderId,\n            String wmsReferenceId,\n            FulfillmentStatus status,\n            String trackingNumber,\n            String carrier,\n            Instant lastUpdated,\n            List<FulfillmentEvent> events\n    ) {}\n\n    /**\n     * Individual fulfillment milestone event.\n     */\n    record FulfillmentEvent(\n            FulfillmentStatus status,\n            String description,\n            Instant occurredAt\n    ) {}\n\n    /**\n     * Possible fulfillment states.\n     */\n    enum FulfillmentStatus {\n        RECEIVED,\n        PROCESSING,\n        PICKING,\n        PACKING,\n        SHIPPED,\n        DELIVERED,\n        CANCELED,\n        ON_HOLD,\n        FAILED\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * WMS health check result.\n     */\n    record WmsHealth(\n            HealthState state,\n            String description,\n            Duration responseTime,\n            Instant checkedAt\n    ) {\n        public enum HealthState { OK, DEGRADED, DOWN }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * WMS integration metadata.\n     */\n    record WmsMetadata(\n            String vendor,\n            String apiVersion,\n            String endpointUrl\n    ) {}\n\n    /* ========================================================================\n     * Exception\n     * ======================================================================== */\n\n    /**\n     * Exception for WMS integration failures.\n     */\n    class WmsIntegrationException extends RuntimeException {\n        private final ErrorCategory category;\n\n        public WmsIntegrationException(String message, ErrorCategory category) {\n            super(message);\n            this.category = category;\n        }\n\n        public WmsIntegrationException(String message, Throwable cause, ErrorCategory category) {\n            super(message, cause);\n            this.category = category;\n        }\n\n        public ErrorCategory category() {\n            return category;\n        }\n\n        public enum ErrorCategory {\n            NETWORK,\n            AUTHENTICATION,\n            VALIDATION,\n            RATE_LIMIT,\n            WMS_REJECTION,\n            TIMEOUT,\n            OTHER\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 2.2 Inbound Port: `UpdateStockUseCase` (Driving Port)\n\n**Location:** `sprintcart-pro-domain/src/main/java/com/sprintcart/domain/ports/in/inventory/UpdateStockUseCase.java`\n\nThis is a **Use Case** port (inbound/driving) that defines the contract for updating stock levels. The application service implements this interface; the WMS adapter's controller calls it.\n\n```java\npackage com.sprintcart.domain.ports.in.inventory;\n\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Objects;\nimport java.util.UUID;\n\n/**\n * Inbound port (Use Case) for handling stock level updates from external systems.\n * <p>\n * This interface is technology-agnostic and must be implemented by an application\n * service. External adapters (REST controllers, message consumers) invoke this\n * port to update inventory levels in the domain.\n *\n * <p><strong>Idempotency:</strong> Implementations must support idempotent operations\n * using the provided {@code correlationId} to prevent duplicate processing.\n */\npublic interface UpdateStockUseCase {\n\n    /**\n     * Processes a batch of stock updates from an external source (e.g., WMS).\n     *\n     * @param command Contains stock adjustments and metadata.\n     * @return Result indicating success/failure for each SKU.\n     * @throws InvalidStockUpdateException if validation fails.\n     */\n    StockUpdateResult updateStock(UpdateStockCommand command)\n            throws InvalidStockUpdateException;\n\n    /**\n     * Processes a single SKU stock update.\n     *\n     * @param command Single SKU update details.\n     * @return Result for the single update.\n     */\n    SingleSkuUpdateResult updateSingleSku(SingleSkuUpdateCommand command)\n            throws InvalidStockUpdateException;\n\n    /* ========================================================================\n     * Command Objects\n     * ======================================================================== */\n\n    /**\n     * Command for batch stock updates.\n     */\n    record UpdateStockCommand(\n            String correlationId,\n            String sourceSystem,\n            Instant receivedAt,\n            List<StockAdjustment> adjustments\n    ) {\n        public UpdateStockCommand {\n            Objects.requireNonNull(correlationId, \"correlationId must not be null\");\n            Objects.requireNonNull(sourceSystem, \"sourceSystem must not be null\");\n            Objects.requireNonNull(adjustments, \"adjustments must not be null\");\n            if (adjustments.isEmpty()) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"adjustments must not be empty\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Command for single SKU update.\n     */\n    record SingleSkuUpdateCommand(\n            String correlationId,\n            String sourceSystem,\n            String sku,\n            int newQuantity,\n            String warehouseLocation,\n            StockUpdateReason reason,\n            Instant effectiveAt\n    ) {\n        public SingleSkuUpdateCommand {\n            Objects.requireNonNull(correlationId, \"correlationId must not be null\");\n            Objects.requireNonNull(sku, \"sku must not be null\");\n            Objects.requireNonNull(reason, \"reason must not be null\");\n            if (newQuantity < 0) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"newQuantity cannot be negative\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Individual stock adjustment within a batch.\n     */\n    record StockAdjustment(\n            String sku,\n            int previousQuantity,\n            int newQuantity,\n            String warehouseLocation,\n            StockUpdateReason reason\n    ) {\n        public StockAdjustment {\n            Objects.requireNonNull(sku, \"sku must not be null\");\n            Objects.requireNonNull(reason, \"reason must not be null\");\n            if (newQuantity < 0) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"newQuantity cannot be negative\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Reason for stock adjustment.\n     */\n    enum StockUpdateReason {\n        WMS_SYNC,\n        GOODS_RECEIVED,\n        RETURN_PROCESSED,\n        DAMAGED,\n        CYCLE_COUNT,\n        SHRINKAGE,\n        MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT,\n        TRANSFER\n    }\n\n    /* ========================================================================\n     * Result Objects\n     * ======================================================================== */\n\n    /**\n     * Result of batch stock update.\n     */\n    record StockUpdateResult(\n            String correlationId,\n            int totalProcessed,\n            int successCount,\n            int failureCount,\n            List<SkuUpdateStatus> details,\n            Instant processedAt\n    ) {}\n\n    /**\n     * Result for single SKU update.\n     */\n    record SingleSkuUpdateResult(\n            String correlationId,\n            String sku,\n            boolean success,\n            String message,\n            int finalQuantity,\n            Instant processedAt\n    ) {}\n\n    /**\n     * Status for individual SKU within batch.\n     */\n    record SkuUpdateStatus(\n            String sku,\n            boolean success,\n            String errorMessage,\n            int finalQuantity\n    ) {}\n\n    /* ========================================================================\n     * Exceptions\n     * ======================================================================== */\n\n    /**\n     * Thrown when stock update validation fails.\n     */\n    class InvalidStockUpdateException extends Exception {\n        public InvalidStockUpdateException(String message) {\n            super(message);\n        }\n\n        public InvalidStockUpdateException(String message, Throwable cause) {\n            super(message, cause);\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Adapter Implementation Plan\n\n### 3.1 Package Structure for `wms-adapter`\n\n```\nwms-adapter/\n└── src/main/java/com/sprintcart/adapters/wms/\n    ├── config/\n    │   ├── WmsAdapterConfiguration.java    // Spring configuration\n    │   ├── WmsSecurityConfiguration.java   // Webhook auth config\n    │   └── WmsClientProperties.java        // @ConfigurationProperties\n    │\n    ├── inbound/                            // Driving adapters (WMS -> SprintCart)\n    │   ├── WmsWebhookController.java       // REST endpoint for WMS callbacks\n    │   ├── dto/\n    │   │   ├── StockUpdateWebhookDto.java\n    │   │   └── FulfillmentStatusWebhookDto.java\n    │   └── mapper/\n    │       └── WmsInboundMapper.java\n    │\n    ├── outbound/                           // Driven adapters (SprintCart -> WMS)\n    │   ├── WmsRestClient.java              // Implements WmsPort\n    │   ├── dto/\n    │   │   ├── WmsFulfillmentRequestDto.java\n    │   │   ├── WmsFulfillmentResponseDto.java\n    │   │   └── WmsStatusResponseDto.java\n    │   └── mapper/\n    │       └── WmsOutboundMapper.java\n    │\n    └── event/\n        └── OrderPlacedEventListener.java   // Subscribes to OrderPlacedEvent\n```\n\n### 3.2 Outbound Adapter: `WmsRestClient`\n\n**Location:** `wms-adapter/src/main/java/com/sprintcart/adapters/wms/outbound/WmsRestClient.java`\n\n**Key Responsibilities:**\n- Implements `WmsPort` interface from domain\n- Uses `WebClient` for non-blocking HTTP calls\n- Applies resilience patterns (circuit breaker, retry, timeout)\n- Handles authentication (API key, OAuth2, etc.)\n- Maps domain objects to/from WMS API DTOs\n\n```java\npackage com.sprintcart.adapters.wms.outbound;\n\nimport com.sprintcart.adapters.wms.config.WmsClientProperties;\nimport com.sprintcart.adapters.wms.outbound.dto.*;\nimport com.sprintcart.adapters.wms.outbound.mapper.WmsOutboundMapper;\nimport com.sprintcart.domain.ports.out.wms.WmsPort;\nimport io.github.resilience4j.circuitbreaker.annotation.CircuitBreaker;\nimport io.github.resilience4j.retry.annotation.Retry;\nimport io.github.resilience4j.timelimiter.annotation.TimeLimiter;\nimport lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;\nimport lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;\nimport org.springframework.stereotype.Component;\nimport org.springframework.web.reactive.function.client.WebClient;\n\nimport java.time.Clock;\nimport java.time.Duration;\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport java.util.UUID;\nimport java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;\n\n/**\n * Outbound adapter implementing {@link WmsPort} for WMS API communication.\n * <p>\n * This adapter is responsible for:\n * <ul>\n *     <li>Translating domain objects to WMS API DTOs</li>\n *     <li>Making HTTP calls to the WMS REST API</li>\n *     <li>Handling errors and applying resilience patterns</li>\n *     <li>Translating WMS responses back to domain objects</li>\n * </ul>\n */\n@Slf4j\n@Component\n@RequiredArgsConstructor\npublic class WmsRestClient implements WmsPort {\n\n    private final WebClient wmsWebClient;\n    private final WmsClientProperties properties;\n    private final WmsOutboundMapper mapper;\n    private final Clock clock;\n\n    @Override\n    @CircuitBreaker(name = \"wms\", fallbackMethod = \"submitFulfillmentFallback\")\n    @Retry(name = \"wms\")\n    @TimeLimiter(name = \"wms\")\n    public CompletableFuture<FulfillmentAcknowledgment> submitFulfillmentRequest(\n            FulfillmentRequest request) {\n\n        log.info(\"Submitting fulfillment request to WMS for order {}\", request.orderId());\n\n        WmsFulfillmentRequestDto dto = mapper.toWmsRequest(request);\n\n        return wmsWebClient.post()\n                .uri(\"/v1/fulfillment/orders\")\n                .header(\"X-Idempotency-Key\", request.correlationId())\n                .header(\"X-API-Key\", properties.getApiKey())\n                .bodyValue(dto)\n                .retrieve()\n                .bodyToMono(WmsFulfillmentResponseDto.class)\n                .map(mapper::toAcknowledgment)\n                .doOnSuccess(ack -> log.info(\"WMS accepted fulfillment for order {}, WMS ref: {}\",\n                        request.orderId(), ack.wmsReferenceId()))\n                .doOnError(ex -> log.error(\"WMS fulfillment submission failed for order {}\",\n                        request.orderId(), ex))\n                .toFuture();\n    }\n\n    // Fallback method for circuit breaker\n    private CompletableFuture<FulfillmentAcknowledgment> submitFulfillmentFallback(\n            FulfillmentRequest request, Throwable throwable) {\n\n        log.warn(\"WMS circuit breaker open, returning degraded response for order {}\",\n                request.orderId());\n        throw new WmsIntegrationException(\n                \"WMS service unavailable\",\n                throwable,\n                WmsIntegrationException.ErrorCategory.NETWORK);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    @CircuitBreaker(name = \"wms\")\n    public CompletableFuture<FulfillmentStatusResponse> getFulfillmentStatus(UUID orderId) {\n        return wmsWebClient.get()\n                .uri(\"/v1/fulfillment/orders/{orderId}/status\", orderId)\n                .header(\"X-API-Key\", properties.getApiKey())\n                .retrieve()\n                .bodyToMono(WmsStatusResponseDto.class)\n                .map(mapper::toStatusResponse)\n                .toFuture();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    @CircuitBreaker(name = \"wms\")\n    public CompletableFuture<Boolean> cancelFulfillment(UUID orderId, String reason) {\n        return wmsWebClient.post()\n                .uri(\"/v1/fulfillment/orders/{orderId}/cancel\", orderId)\n                .header(\"X-API-Key\", properties.getApiKey())\n                .bodyValue(new CancelRequestDto(reason))\n                .retrieve()\n                .toBodilessEntity()\n                .map(response -> response.getStatusCode().is2xxSuccessful())\n                .toFuture();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public WmsHealth healthCheck() {\n        Instant start = clock.instant();\n        try {\n            Boolean healthy = wmsWebClient.get()\n                    .uri(\"/health\")\n                    .retrieve()\n                    .toBodilessEntity()\n                    .map(r -> r.getStatusCode().is2xxSuccessful())\n                    .block(Duration.ofSeconds(5));\n\n            return new WmsHealth(\n                    Boolean.TRUE.equals(healthy) ? WmsHealth.HealthState.OK : WmsHealth.HealthState.DEGRADED,\n                    \"WMS API responded\",\n                    Duration.between(start, clock.instant()),\n                    clock.instant()\n            );\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            return new WmsHealth(\n                    WmsHealth.HealthState.DOWN,\n                    \"WMS API unreachable: \" + e.getMessage(),\n                    Duration.between(start, clock.instant()),\n                    clock.instant()\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public WmsMetadata metadata() {\n        return new WmsMetadata(\n                properties.getVendor(),\n                properties.getApiVersion(),\n                properties.getBaseUrl()\n        );\n    }\n\n    // Internal DTO for cancel request\n    private record CancelRequestDto(String reason) {}\n}\n```\n\n### 3.3 Inbound Adapter: `WmsWebhookController`\n\n**Location:** `wms-adapter/src/main/java/com/sprintcart/adapters/wms/inbound/WmsWebhookController.java`\n\n**Key Responsibilities:**\n- Exposes REST endpoint(s) for WMS webhook callbacks\n- Validates webhook signatures for security\n- Maps incoming DTOs to use case commands\n- Delegates to application layer (not domain directly)\n- Returns appropriate HTTP responses\n\n```java\npackage com.sprintcart.adapters.wms.inbound;\n\nimport com.sprintcart.adapters.wms.inbound.dto.StockUpdateWebhookDto;\nimport com.sprintcart.adapters.wms.inbound.mapper.WmsInboundMapper;\nimport com.sprintcart.domain.ports.in.inventory.UpdateStockUseCase;\nimport com.sprintcart.domain.ports.in.inventory.UpdateStockUseCase.*;\nimport jakarta.validation.Valid;\nimport lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;\nimport lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;\nimport org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;\nimport org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;\nimport org.springframework.validation.annotation.Validated;\nimport org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;\n\nimport java.time.Instant;\n\n/**\n * Inbound adapter (REST controller) that handles WMS webhook callbacks.\n * <p>\n * This controller is deliberately thin - it validates requests, maps DTOs to\n * domain commands, and delegates all business logic to the application layer\n * via the {@link UpdateStockUseCase} port.\n *\n * <h3>Security</h3>\n * Endpoints are secured via HMAC signature validation configured in\n * {@code WmsSecurityConfiguration}. The WMS must sign requests with a\n * shared secret.\n */\n@Slf4j\n@RestController\n@RequestMapping(\"/api/v1/wms/webhooks\")\n@RequiredArgsConstructor\n@Validated\npublic class WmsWebhookController {\n\n    private final UpdateStockUseCase updateStockUseCase;\n    private final WmsInboundMapper mapper;\n\n    /**\n     * Endpoint for WMS to push stock level updates.\n     * <p>\n     * The WMS calls this endpoint whenever inventory levels change\n     * (goods received, shipments, cycle counts, etc.).\n     *\n     * @param signature    HMAC signature from X-WMS-Signature header\n     * @param webhookId    Unique webhook event ID for idempotency\n     * @param payload      Stock update payload from WMS\n     * @return Acknowledgment with processing results\n     */\n    @PostMapping(\"/stock-updates\")\n    public ResponseEntity<StockUpdateResponseDto> handleStockUpdate(\n            @RequestHeader(value = \"X-WMS-Signature\", required = true) String signature,\n            @RequestHeader(value = \"X-WMS-Webhook-Id\", required = true) String webhookId,\n            @Valid @RequestBody StockUpdateWebhookDto payload) {\n\n        log.info(\"Received stock update webhook from WMS. Webhook ID: {}, SKU count: {}\",\n                webhookId, payload.getAdjustments().size());\n\n        try {\n            // Map DTO to domain command\n            UpdateStockCommand command = mapper.toCommand(payload, webhookId);\n\n            // Delegate to application layer use case\n            StockUpdateResult result = updateStockUseCase.updateStock(command);\n\n            log.info(\"Stock update processed. Webhook ID: {}, Success: {}/{}, Failures: {}\",\n                    webhookId, result.successCount(), result.totalProcessed(), result.failureCount());\n\n            return ResponseEntity.ok(new StockUpdateResponseDto(\n                    webhookId,\n                    result.successCount(),\n                    result.failureCount(),\n                    result.processedAt()\n            ));\n\n        } catch (InvalidStockUpdateException e) {\n            log.warn(\"Invalid stock update from WMS. Webhook ID: {}, Error: {}\",\n                    webhookId, e.getMessage());\n            return ResponseEntity.badRequest()\n                    .body(new StockUpdateResponseDto(webhookId, 0, payload.getAdjustments().size(),\n                            Instant.now(), e.getMessage()));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Endpoint for WMS to push fulfillment status updates.\n     * <p>\n     * Called when fulfillment status changes (picked, packed, shipped, etc.).\n     */\n    @PostMapping(\"/fulfillment-status\")\n    public ResponseEntity<Void> handleFulfillmentStatus(\n            @RequestHeader(value = \"X-WMS-Signature\", required = true) String signature,\n            @RequestHeader(value = \"X-WMS-Webhook-Id\", required = true) String webhookId,\n            @Valid @RequestBody FulfillmentStatusWebhookDto payload) {\n\n        log.info(\"Received fulfillment status update from WMS. Order: {}, Status: {}\",\n                payload.getOrderId(), payload.getStatus());\n\n        // Delegate to appropriate use case for status update handling\n        // This would update order status and emit domain events\n\n        return ResponseEntity.accepted().build();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Health check endpoint for WMS to verify connectivity.\n     */\n    @GetMapping(\"/health\")\n    public ResponseEntity<String> healthCheck() {\n        return ResponseEntity.ok(\"OK\");\n    }\n\n    // Response DTOs\n\n    public record StockUpdateResponseDto(\n            String webhookId,\n            int successCount,\n            int failureCount,\n            Instant processedAt,\n            String errorMessage\n    ) {\n        public StockUpdateResponseDto(String webhookId, int successCount, int failureCount, Instant processedAt) {\n            this(webhookId, successCount, failureCount, processedAt, null);\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 3.4 Event Listener: `OrderPlacedEventListener`\n\n**Location:** `wms-adapter/src/main/java/com/sprintcart/adapters/wms/event/OrderPlacedEventListener.java`\n\nThis component subscribes to `OrderPlacedEvent` domain events and triggers WMS fulfillment requests.\n\n```java\npackage com.sprintcart.adapters.wms.event;\n\nimport com.sprintcart.application.service.DomainEventProcessor.DomainEvent;\nimport com.sprintcart.application.service.DomainEventProcessor.DomainEventSubscriber;\nimport com.sprintcart.domain.events.OrderPlacedEvent;\nimport com.sprintcart.domain.ports.out.wms.WmsPort;\nimport com.sprintcart.domain.ports.out.wms.WmsPort.*;\nimport lombok.NonNull;\nimport lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;\nimport lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;\nimport org.springframework.stereotype.Component;\n\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport java.util.stream.Collectors;\n\n/**\n * Domain event subscriber that listens for {@link OrderPlacedEvent}s and\n * triggers fulfillment requests to the WMS.\n * <p>\n * This follows the event-driven pattern established by the existing\n * {@link com.sprintcart.application.service.DomainEventProcessor}.\n */\n@Slf4j\n@Component\n@RequiredArgsConstructor\npublic class OrderPlacedEventListener implements DomainEventSubscriber<OrderPlacedEvent> {\n\n    private final WmsPort wmsPort;\n\n    @Override\n    public void handle(@NonNull OrderPlacedEvent event) {\n        log.info(\"Processing OrderPlacedEvent for order {}\", event.getOrderId());\n\n        FulfillmentRequest request = mapToFulfillmentRequest(event);\n\n        wmsPort.submitFulfillmentRequest(request)\n                .thenAccept(ack -> log.info(\"WMS acknowledged fulfillment for order {}. WMS Ref: {}\",\n                        event.getOrderId(), ack.wmsReferenceId()))\n                .exceptionally(ex -> {\n                    log.error(\"Failed to submit fulfillment to WMS for order {}\",\n                            event.getOrderId(), ex);\n                    // In production: publish to DLQ or retry queue\n                    return null;\n                });\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean supports(@NonNull Class<? extends DomainEvent> eventType) {\n        return OrderPlacedEvent.class.equals(eventType);\n    }\n\n    private FulfillmentRequest mapToFulfillmentRequest(OrderPlacedEvent event) {\n        var lineItems = event.getOrderLines().stream()\n                .map(line -> new FulfillmentLineItem(\n                        line.getProductId(),\n                        line.getSku(),\n                        line.getSku(), // Product name would come from enrichment\n                        line.getQuantity(),\n                        null\n                ))\n                .collect(Collectors.toList());\n\n        // ShippingDetails would be fetched from Order aggregate or enriched\n        ShippingDetails shippingDetails = new ShippingDetails(\n                \"Customer Name\", // Would come from order/customer service\n                \"Address Line 1\",\n                null,\n                \"City\",\n                \"State\",\n                \"12345\",\n                \"US\",\n                null,\n                \"STANDARD\"\n        );\n\n        return new FulfillmentRequest(\n                event.getOrderId(),\n                event.getCustomerId(),\n                event.getCorrelationId() != null ? event.getCorrelationId() : event.eventId().toString(),\n                shippingDetails,\n                lineItems,\n                FulfillmentPriority.STANDARD,\n                Instant.now()\n        );\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Application Layer Orchestration\n\n### 4.1 Modifications to `CatalogService`\n\nThe `CatalogService` in `sprintcart-pro-application` should implement the `UpdateStockUseCase` interface to handle inbound stock updates from the WMS.\n\n**Location:** `sprintcart-pro-application/src/main/java/com/sprintcart/application/service/CatalogService.java`\n\n**Required Changes:**\n\n```java\npackage com.sprintcart.application.service;\n\n// ... existing imports ...\nimport com.sprintcart.domain.ports.in.inventory.UpdateStockUseCase;\n\n/**\n * Application service that orchestrates catalog-related use-cases.\n * Now also implements UpdateStockUseCase for WMS stock updates.\n */\n@Slf4j\n@Service\n@RequiredArgsConstructor\npublic class CatalogService implements UpdateStockUseCase {\n\n    private final CatalogPort catalogPort;\n    private final AdvertisementPort advertisementPort;\n    private final EventPublisher eventPublisher;\n    private final Validator validator;\n    private final Clock clock;\n\n    // ... existing methods ...\n\n    /* ========================================================================\n     * UpdateStockUseCase Implementation (for WMS integration)\n     * ======================================================================== */\n\n    @Override\n    @Transactional\n    public StockUpdateResult updateStock(UpdateStockCommand command) throws InvalidStockUpdateException {\n        log.info(\"Processing batch stock update. Correlation: {}, Adjustments: {}\",\n                command.correlationId(), command.adjustments().size());\n\n        // Check for duplicate processing (idempotency)\n        if (isAlreadyProcessed(command.correlationId())) {\n            log.info(\"Duplicate stock update ignored. Correlation: {}\", command.correlationId());\n            return getPreviousResult(command.correlationId());\n        }\n\n        List<SkuUpdateStatus> results = new ArrayList<>();\n        int successCount = 0;\n\n        for (StockAdjustment adjustment : command.adjustments()) {\n            try {\n                updateProductStock(adjustment);\n                results.add(new SkuUpdateStatus(adjustment.sku(), true, null, adjustment.newQuantity()));\n                successCount++;\n\n                // Check for low stock and trigger automations\n                checkLowStockThreshold(adjustment.sku(), adjustment.newQuantity());\n\n            } catch (Exception e) {\n                log.warn(\"Failed to update stock for SKU {}. Error: {}\", adjustment.sku(), e.getMessage());\n                results.add(new SkuUpdateStatus(adjustment.sku(), false, e.getMessage(), -1));\n            }\n        }\n\n        StockUpdateResult result = new StockUpdateResult(\n                command.correlationId(),\n                command.adjustments().size(),\n                successCount,\n                command.adjustments().size() - successCount,\n                results,\n                Instant.now(clock)\n        );\n\n        // Store result for idempotency checks\n        storeProcessedResult(command.correlationId(), result);\n\n        log.info(\"Batch stock update completed. Success: {}, Failures: {}\",\n                result.successCount(), result.failureCount());\n\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    @Transactional\n    public SingleSkuUpdateResult updateSingleSku(SingleSkuUpdateCommand command)\n            throws InvalidStockUpdateException {\n        log.info(\"Processing single SKU stock update. SKU: {}, New Qty: {}\",\n                command.sku(), command.newQuantity());\n\n        try {\n            StockAdjustment adjustment = new StockAdjustment(\n                    command.sku(),\n                    -1, // Previous quantity unknown for single update\n                    command.newQuantity(),\n                    command.warehouseLocation(),\n                    command.reason()\n            );\n\n            updateProductStock(adjustment);\n            checkLowStockThreshold(command.sku(), command.newQuantity());\n\n            return new SingleSkuUpdateResult(\n                    command.correlationId(),\n                    command.sku(),\n                    true,\n                    \"Stock updated successfully\",\n                    command.newQuantity(),\n                    Instant.now(clock)\n            );\n\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            throw new InvalidStockUpdateException(\"Failed to update SKU \" + command.sku() + \": \" + e.getMessage(), e);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void updateProductStock(StockAdjustment adjustment) {\n        // Use existing CatalogPort to update product inventory\n        catalogPort.updateStockBySku(adjustment.sku(), adjustment.newQuantity(), adjustment.warehouseLocation());\n    }\n\n    private void checkLowStockThreshold(String sku, int newQuantity) {\n        // Emit LowStockEvent if below threshold (existing logic)\n        catalogPort.findBySku(sku).ifPresent(product -> {\n            product.getInventory().ifPresent(inventory -> {\n                if (newQuantity < inventory.reorderThreshold()) {\n                    log.info(\"Low stock detected for SKU {}. Qty: {}, Threshold: {}\",\n                            sku, newQuantity, inventory.reorderThreshold());\n                    advertisementPort.pauseCampaignsForProduct(product.getId());\n                    eventPublisher.publishAsync(new LowStockEvent(\n                            product.getId(), newQuantity, inventory.reorderThreshold(), Instant.now(clock)));\n                }\n            });\n        });\n    }\n\n    // Idempotency helpers (would use Redis/database in production)\n    private boolean isAlreadyProcessed(String correlationId) {\n        // Implementation would check persistent store\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private StockUpdateResult getPreviousResult(String correlationId) {\n        // Implementation would retrieve from persistent store\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    private void storeProcessedResult(String correlationId, StockUpdateResult result) {\n        // Implementation would persist for idempotency\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 4.2 Sequence of Calls\n\n#### Outbound Flow: Order Placed -> WMS Fulfillment\n\n```\n1. Customer completes checkout\n2. OrderController.createOrder() is called\n3. OrderService.placeOrder() persists order and publishes OrderPlacedEvent\n4. DomainEventProcessor.process() receives the event\n5. OrderPlacedEventListener.handle() is invoked\n6. OrderPlacedEventListener maps event to FulfillmentRequest\n7. WmsPort.submitFulfillmentRequest() is called\n8. WmsRestClient (adapter) makes HTTP POST to WMS API\n9. WMS responds with FulfillmentAcknowledgment\n10. Listener logs success (or handles failure)\n```\n\n#### Inbound Flow: WMS Stock Update -> SprintCart\n\n```\n1. WMS detects inventory change (goods received, shipment, etc.)\n2. WMS calls POST /api/v1/wms/webhooks/stock-updates\n3. WmsWebhookController validates signature and parses request\n4. Controller maps DTO to UpdateStockCommand\n5. Controller calls UpdateStockUseCase.updateStock()\n6. CatalogService (implementing use case) processes each adjustment\n7. CatalogPort.updateStockBySku() updates database\n8. Low stock check triggers LowStockEvent if needed\n9. Controller returns StockUpdateResponseDto to WMS\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Data Flow Diagram: Inbound Stock Update\n\n### 5.1 End-to-End Component Flow\n\n```\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                           EXTERNAL: WMS System                                │\n│                                                                               │\n│    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│    │  WMS detects inventory change (goods received, cycle count, etc.)   │   │\n│    └─────────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                          │                                    │\n│                                          ▼                                    │\n│    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│    │  WMS signs payload with HMAC using shared secret                    │   │\n│    │  POST https://sprintcart.com/api/v1/wms/webhooks/stock-updates      │   │\n│    │  Headers: X-WMS-Signature, X-WMS-Webhook-Id                         │   │\n│    │  Body: { adjustments: [{ sku, previousQty, newQty, reason }] }      │   │\n│    └─────────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘   │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────┘\n                                           │\n                                           │ HTTPS\n                                           ▼\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                     INBOUND ADAPTER: wms-adapter module                       │\n│                                                                               │\n│    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│    │  Step 1: WmsSecurityFilter (Spring Security)                        │   │\n│    │  - Validates X-WMS-Signature header using HMAC-SHA256               │   │\n│    │  - Rejects request with 401 if signature invalid                    │   │\n│    └─────────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                          │                                    │\n│                                          ▼                                    │\n│    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│    │  Step 2: WmsWebhookController.handleStockUpdate()                   │   │\n│    │  - Receives StockUpdateWebhookDto                                   │   │\n│    │  - Validates request body (@Valid)                                  │   │\n│    │  - Logs webhook receipt                                             │   │\n│    └─────────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                          │                                    │\n│                                          ▼                                    │\n│    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│    │  Step 3: WmsInboundMapper.toCommand()                               │   │\n│    │  - Maps StockUpdateWebhookDto -> UpdateStockCommand                 │   │\n│    │  - Converts WMS-specific fields to domain concepts                  │   │\n│    └─────────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                          │                                    │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────┘\n                                           │\n                                           │ Calls Use Case Port\n                                           ▼\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                APPLICATION LAYER: sprintcart-pro-application                  │\n│                                                                               │\n│    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│    │  Step 4: UpdateStockUseCase.updateStock(command)                    │   │\n│    │  (Implemented by CatalogService)                                    │   │\n│    │                                                                     │   │\n│    │  - Check idempotency (correlationId)                                │   │\n│    │  - Begin transaction                                                │   │\n│    │  - For each StockAdjustment:                                        │   │\n│    │      - Call CatalogPort.updateStockBySku()                          │   │\n│    │      - Check low stock threshold                                    │   │\n│    │      - Emit LowStockEvent if needed                                 │   │\n│    │  - Commit transaction                                               │   │\n│    │  - Store result for idempotency                                     │   │\n│    │  - Return StockUpdateResult                                         │   │\n│    └─────────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                          │                                    │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────┘\n                                           │\n                                           │ Calls Outbound Ports\n                                           ▼\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                       OUTBOUND ADAPTERS & INFRASTRUCTURE                      │\n│                                                                               │\n│    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│    │  Step 5: CatalogPort (ProductRepositoryAdapter)                     │   │\n│    │  - updateStockBySku(sku, newQuantity, location)                     │   │\n│    │  - Executes SQL UPDATE on product_inventory table                   │   │\n│    │  - Uses optimistic locking (@Version)                               │   │\n│    └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                    │   │\n│    │  - Publishes LowStockEvent to domain event bus                      │   │\n│    │  - Triggers automation subscribers                                  │   │\n│    │  - May pause ad campaigns via AdvertisementPort                     │   │\n│    └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                               │\n│    ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│    │  Step 7: ElasticsearchAdapter (search index)                        │   │\n│    │  - Updates product document with new stock level                    │   │\n│    │  - Maintains search index consistency                               │   │\n│    └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                               │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                                           │\n                                           │\n                          │   │\n│    │  - product_inventory table updated                                  │   │\n│    │  - stock_update_log entry created (audit trail)                     │   │\n│
